Additionally, students may also take 2 graduate study courses during their junior and/or senior year and complete the rest of the Master of Education in Special Education requirements within one additional year after completing their Bachelor of Education in Special Education degree.</p> <h3>General Admission Requirements</h3> <ol> <li>Completion of a minimum of 75&nbsp;CH in the Bachelor of Education degree (SPED).</li> <li>A cumulative GPA of 3.5&nbsp;or higher.</li> <li>Proficiency in English demonstrated by a score of 6.0 or higher in&nbsp;IELTS or equivalent.</li> <li>Submission of two Letters of Recommendations.</li> </ol> <h3>The Early Admission Procedure</h3> <ol> <li>Interested students should first check on their eligibility with their respective Graduate Program Coordinator, then submit an application by the announced deadline as specified by the University&rsquo;s Admission office&nbsp;</li> <li>Once the early admission requirements are reviewed by the respective Graduate Program Committee, its recommendation for admission is forwarded to the University&rsquo;s Admission Office for official pre-admission&nbsp;to graduate programs.</li> </ol> <h3>Academic Advising Guidelines</h3> <p>During the first semester, Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option students will confer with the respective Graduate Program Coordinator and faculty members who correlate with his/her research interests.
After potential research areas have been identified and mutually agreed upon with a faculty member and the Graduate Program Committee, the designated faculty member will become the student&rsquo;s academic advisor to recommend and approve his/her course schedule for the rest of the duration of the program.
This academic advisor will also supervise the student&rsquo;s thesis or Master&rsquo;s graduation project.</p> <h3>Probation and Dismissal from the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option Status</h3> <p>All applicable UAEU policies and procedures regarding academic progress of students will apply to the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option student.</p>

DESCRIPTION: The Bachelor of Business Administration in Human Resource Management and Development (BBA_HRMD) degree enables students to pursue a broad range of careers in business and government sectors in the broad area of Human Resources Management and Development. Driven by students’ need to compete in a global job market, the BBA_HRMD program is internationally accredited providing students with worldwide recognition of their prestigious academic degrees. The program is designed to help meet the growing and changing labor market needs of the UAE economy. The BBA in HRMD curriculum equips students with core business skills and knowledge in all business functions including finance, accounting, and economics. Additionally, specialized courses in the program prepare graduates with specialist knowledge and skills in various areas in the human resource management and development field, including strategies and tools to attract, train, engage, retain, and motivate employees in the workplace. Students obtain a solid foundation in management theories and analytical skills, complemented by practical exposure to real-world business practices through the internship program. The program prepares students not only for careers in government and industry but also for graduate studies. The current BBA in HRMD program is a proactive response to the evolving needs of the UAE and regional markets. The program provides students with diverse opportunities to prepare for careers in today's dynamic and competitive business environment by offering comprehensive and up-to-date knowledge and skills in various aspects of people management within organizations. This includes the internship program, wherein all students complete a compulsory work-placement in an organization. This initiative has proven to be extremely beneficial to our graduates, equipping them with practical skills and professional insights. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).
STUDYPLAN_URL: https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cbe/pdf/cbe_bsc_business_adminstration_hrmd_study_plan.pdf

DESCRIPTION: The Bachelor of Business Administration in Entrepreneurship corresponds with the UAE government's vision for establishing the nation as an entrepreneurial nation. This program introduces students with entrepreneurship as a multifaceted discipline, integrating innovation and creativity to cultivate a problem-solving mindset. The curriculum equips students for entrepreneurship by teaching social and business principles and leading them through the process of product and service conception to commercialization. The program leverages cutting-edge resources at the UAEU, including the Science and Innovation Park and the Emirates Centre for Entrepreneurship Research, to facilitate the entrepreneurial process from ideation to the commercialization of students' ventures into business. The program recognizes global entrepreneurial opportunities and prepares students for pursuing international business opportunities. This curriculum integrates theoretical knowledge with practical application, equipping students to succeed in entrepreneurship and also avail corporate positions, fostering innovation and sustainability in a competitive global market. In summary, students acquire both theoretical and practical knowledge in individual and corporate entrepreneurship, learning to identify sustainable business opportunities, evaluate business prospects, and foster collaborative innovation. The program adopts a multidisciplinary approach, examining viewpoints from economics, psychology, sociology, and geography. It underscores the entrepreneur's engagement with society, accentuating the social and cultural elements that drive entrepreneurial success. ## Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Geosciences — published program-finder record
Record program: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Geosciences
Record college: College of Science
Record qualification: accelerated
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/bachelormaster-accelerated-option-in-geosciences
TITLE: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Geosciences
TITLE_AR: خيار البكالوريوس-الماجستير المسرع في علوم الأرض
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Science
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية العلوم
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Geosciences
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الجيولوجيا
PROGRAM_ID: 22784
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Geosciences gives a chance for talented students to progress in their careers through the engagement in graduate courses and thesis research during their last year of undergraduate (BSc) studies at the Department of Geosciences.
Students enrolled in the BS-MS program in Geosciences are allowed to register for up to four (4) MSc courses at the 500-level or 600-level during their fourth year 4 of BSc studies and their first year at the BS-MS program.
Two (2), of these courses will be double-counted towards their BSc degree and MSc degree.
During year 5 of the BS-MS, the enrolled student will have to register for the remaining courses of the MSc degree, including the COS-required courses and the Thesis research hours.
Students involved in employment after getting their BSc degree may be advised to continue their MSc degree as part-time students, hence their BS-MS duration may be extended to a total of 6 years.
Accordingly, a BS-MS student will normally earn an MSc degree after finishing the 120 CH for the BSc and the requirements for the MSc with a total of 144 CH.&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</p> <h3>Program Objectives</h3> <ul> <li>Shorten the study period to earn graduate degrees for outstanding students, as they may be able to complete their MSc degree within 1-1.5 additional years after the BSc instead of the typical 2-3 years in the regular admission route.</li> <li>Make it easier for BSc students to continue on to graduate studies to provide them with advanced knowledge, skills, and attributes that will make them ready for successful careers at the forefront of academic and industrial development.</li> <li>Better prepare the Geosciences graduates for the current and future requirements of professional registration within the UAE and internationally, for which having an MSc would be an advantage.</li> <li>Foster a tradition of graduate research activity in the Department of Geosciences with the expectation that there will be an additional benefit to the undergraduate program.</li> </ul> <h3>Launching Requirements</h3> <p>The proposed BS-MS program depends on the currently available BSc (Geosciences) and MSc (Geosciences) programs, where courses and instructors of both programs will be available to teach in the newly proposed BS-MS program.
No further logistic or manpower requirements are needed at this stage.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p> <h3>Credit Requirements</h3> <p>The current BSc degrees in Geosciences typically requires the completion of 120 credit hours.&nbsp;After the completion of these credit hours, Geosciences BSc graduates can apply for an MSc degree in Geosciences by completing an additional 30 credit hours of graduate course work.

## Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Geosciences — published program-finder record — continuation 2
Record program: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Geosciences
Record college: College of Science
Record qualification: accelerated
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/bachelormaster-accelerated-option-in-geosciences
For the proposed integrated BS-MS program, outstanding senior undergraduate students will be allowed to choose up to 6 credit hours of undergraduate electives from the 500-level or 600-level Geosciences graduate courses (electives) with double counting of such credit hours towards their BSc and MSc degrees.
This will allow students to obtain their MSc degree after their BSc by completing the remaining 24 credit hours of graduate course work.&nbsp; A total of 144 CHs will be sufficient for talented students to obtain integrated BSc and MSc degrees in a maximum of 5.5 years for full time students and could be extended to 6 years for part-time students.
The double counted courses (6 CH) have been selected to contribute to the efficient employability of the BS-MS graduates.
These include GEOL521 (Geochemistry for Environment and Mineral Exploration) and GEOL532 (Non-Seismic Methods).
These courses must be taken by the BSC-MSc students as free elective (6 CH) in the non-Geosciences basket.
The BS-MS students will not pay fees for the double counted courses if their BS tuition fees are paid by the Federal Government because the courses are part of the requirements for BS degree.
Students interested in the new BS-MS program will be advised after successful completion of 75 CH to take a track oriented towards graduate study.</p> <h3>General Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>Junior or Senior BSc standing, with at least 75 CH completed and a minimum GPA of 3.5</li> <li>Official program entry requires 75&nbsp;CH completed with a 3.5 GPA or higher.</li> <li>A minimum score of 6.0 in IELTS or equivalent that demonstrates proficiency in English.</li> <li>Acceptance of the obligation and responsibility for the graduate study fees.</li> <li>A statement of professional goals.</li> </ul> <h3>Competitive Admission</h3> <ul> <li>5 to10 students per cohort per semester ensures rigorous academic standards and maximizes individual guidance and support.</li> <li>When there are class limits within the graduate programs, admission will be processed on a competitive basis based on the student&rsquo;s GPA and career objectives.</li> </ul> <h3>Mode of Enrollment</h3> <p>Students will be enrolled as full-time, face-to-face BS-MS students.
However, part-time students will be also given the chance to apply to the program so that their overall duration of the BS-MS program is extended to six (6) years.
Admission will be made available every semester, but could be limited to Fall semester of every academic year depending on the rate of admission and the capacity of the Department of Geosciences</p> <h3>The Early Admission Procedure</h3> <p>Interested students should first check on their eligibility with the Graduate Program Coordinator at the Department of Geosciences, then submit an application as specified by the University&rsquo;s Admission office to the Departmental Chairperson by the announced deadline.

Such an application should be simpler than the regular graduate program application, as the student information is already on file with the UAEU Registrar&rsquo;s office.</p> <p>Once it is reviewed that the early admission requirements are fulfilled by the student and the student is recommended by the Graduate Program Committee at the Department of Geosciences, the department&rsquo;s decision that the student can be enrolled in the BS-MS program will be forwarded to the University&rsquo;s Admission Office for official pre-admission by the announced university deadlines for regular admission to graduate programs.</p> <h3>Academic Advising Guidelines</h3> <p>During the first semester, the BS-MS student will confer with the Graduate Program Coordinator and faculty members who correlate with his/her research interests.

The applicant may apply for the Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option&nbsp;after successful completion of 75 CH, however, he/she can only join the Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option after successfully completing a minimum of 75&nbsp;CH in the Bachelor degree program with a cumulative GPA of 3.5 or higher.</li> <li>Proficiency in English demonstrated by a score of 6.0 or higher in IELTS or equivalent.</li> <li>Acceptance of the obligation and responsibility for the graduate study fees.</li> <li>A statement of professional goals.</li> </ol> <p>When there are class limits within the graduate programs, admission will be processed on a competitive basis based on the student&rsquo;s GPA and career objectives.</p> <p><u>Modes of Enrollment:</u></p> <p>Students enroll as full-time Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option students.</p> <h3>The Early Admission Procedure</h3> <p>Interested students should first check on their eligibility with their respective Graduate Program Coordinator, then submit an application as specified by the University&rsquo;s Admission office to the College Research and Graduate Studies Office by the announced deadline.</p> <p>Once the requirements are reviewed and recommended by Graduate Program Committee, the application will be forwarded to the University&rsquo;s Admission Office for official pre-admission to graduate programs.</p> <h3>Academic Advising Guidelines</h3> <p>During the first semester, the Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option student will confer with the Graduate Program Coordinator and faculty members who correlate with his/her research interests.
After potential research areas have been identified and mutually agreed to with a faculty member and the Graduate Program Committee, the designated faculty member will become the student&rsquo;s academic advisor to recommend and approve his/her course schedule for the rest of the duration of the program.
This academic advisor will also supervise the student&rsquo;s thesis.</p> <h3>Probation and Dismissal from the Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option Status</h3> <p>All applicable UAEU policies and procedures regarding academic progress of students will apply as relevant for the undergraduate portion of the studies and the graduate portion.

## Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Mathematics — published program-finder record
Record program: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Mathematics
Record college: College of Science
Record qualification: accelerated
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/bachelormaster-accelerated-option-in-mathematics
TITLE: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Mathematics
TITLE_AR: خيار البكالوريوس-الماجستير المسرع في الرياضيات
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Science
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية العلوم
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Mathematical Sciences
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم علوم الرياضيات
PROGRAM_ID: 22837
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Department of Mathematical Sciences offers an excellent opportunity for motivated students to earn both a Bachelor and a Master of Science degree in five years.
This opportunity requires devoted commitment.
By integrating advanced coursework with closely supervised research, the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option broadens opportunities for students by preparing them for PhD studies and employment.
This program offers students a quicker path to complete their targeted MSc degree in mathematics, within one additional year, instead of the current two years after the BSc.
Highly motivated students may apply for the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option after successful completion of 75 CH, however, theycan only join the program after completing a minimum of 75&nbsp;CH in the Bachelor degree program with a cumulative GPA of 3.5&nbsp;or higher.
Students will take upper-level (500) courses at the graduate level, 6 credits of which may count toward both BSc in Mathematics and MSc in Mathematics degrees.
The BSc in Mathematics is accredited by the Applied Science Accreditation&nbsp;Commission of ABET, under the commission&#39;s General Criteria&nbsp;with no applicable criteria.&nbsp;</p> <h3>Objectives</h3> <ol> <li>Make it easier for BSc students to continue their graduate studies.</li> <li>Shorten the study period to earn graduate degrees for outstanding students, as they may be able to complete their MSc degree within one year after the BSc instead of the typical two years in the regular admission route.</li> <li>Foster a tradition of graduate research activity in the Mathematical Sciences Department.</li> </ol> <h3>Credit Requirements</h3> <p>After the achievement of the BSc degree, which requires the completion of 120 CH, students can earn MSc degree by completing an additional 30 CH of graduate course work.
In Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option, excellent undergraduate students will be allowed to choose up to a total of 6 CH from the 500-level Math Graduate Courses with double counting of such credit hours towards their BSc and MSc degrees.
This will allow students to obtain their MSc degree after their BSc by completing the remaining 24 CH of graduate course work.

A total of 144 CH will be sufficient for distinguished students to obtain Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option in 5 years.</p> <h3>General Admission Requirements</h3> <ol> <li>The applicant may apply for the Bachelor-Master&nbsp;Accelerated&nbsp;Option after successful completion of 75 CH; however, she/he can only join the program after successfully completing a minimum of 75&nbsp;CH in the bachelor&#39;s degree program with a cumulative GPA of 3.5&nbsp;or higher.</li> <li>Acceptance of the obligation and responsibility for the graduate study fees.</li> <li>Proficiency in English demonstrated by a score of 6.0 or higher in the IELTS or equivalent.</li> </ol> <h3>The Early Admission Procedure</h3> <ol> <li>Current model study plans for the BSc Mathematical Sciences program average 15-18 CH per semester.

## Bachelor of Science in the Mathematics of Data Science — published program-finder record
Record program: Bachelor of Science in the Mathematics of Data Science
Record college: College of Science
Record qualification: bachelor
Record degreeLevel: undergraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/bachelor-of-science-in-the-mathematics-of-data-science
TITLE: Bachelor of Science in the Mathematics of Data Science
TITLE_AR: بكالوريوس العلوم في رياضيات علم البيانات
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Science
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية العلوم
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Mathematical Sciences
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم علوم الرياضيات
PROGRAM_ID: 22911
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: Critical sectors of the economy and whole fields of inquiry have been transformed, as the availability of data at unprecedented scale has made it possible to solve daunting problems, that were previously inaccessible. This data revolution has fueled a meteoric rise in the demand for data scientists, with advanced analytical skills, who can transform data into knowledge and business insights. The Mathematics of Data Science program is designed to address the increasing talent gap in data science and train the next generation of highly skilled data scientists with the knowledge and domain expertise needed to thrive in a data-driven economy. The Mathematics of Data Science program will be hosted by the Department of Mathematical Sciences, COS. The program emphasizes the integration of the three pillars of data science – computational, and mathematical thinking – into a cohesive transdisciplinary course of study and internship experience in data analytics. The gained knowledge will introduce students to the concepts, tools, and methods to collect, process, and analyze data. Students will develop the ability to uncover hidden patterns and gain useful insights to solve real-world problems. The emphasis on developing and solving real-world problems in the relevant domain, two distinctive features of the program, will enable students to gain a comprehensive and fully integrated literacy in the foundational areas of data science and artificial intelligence and develop expertise in their domain of interest. Armed with these skills, students will develop the ability to use data and methodically connect theory to problems in hand to derive effective solutions to these problems in the context of the domain under consideration. Although a large demand for data science skills comes from high-tech companies, recent reports show that sectors other than technology are facing a noticeable shortage of skilled data scientists. The graduates of the program will gain the core skills and domain expertise to successfully compete for leading-edge jobs in all sectors of the economy. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).
STUDYPLAN_URL: https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cos/pdf/cos_bsc_mathematics_of_data_science_study_plan.pdf

DESCRIPTION: The Bachelor of Science in Speech-Language Pathology is a comprehensive and rigorous program which prepares students for positions in a variety of settings including hospitals, rehabilitation facilities, long-term and extended care facilities, schools, agencies, and private practices. The program trains students on how to understand, diagnose and efficiently treat a number of speech and language disorders throughout the entire lifespan of human development from early childhood through the elder years. These include voice disorders, articulation problems, fluency problems, aphasia, phonological problems, and delays in speech or language. Students gain a core theoretical understanding of a range of communication, swallowing, and related disorders, and discuss principles of assessing, treating, and managing people with communication and swallowing difficulties. The program provides a commitment for academic excellence by incorporating evidence-based practice, problem-based learning, innovative clinical training methods, and interprofessional education and practice. Upon graduation, students will have developed a sufficient level of expertise for safe and competent management of a broad range of patients within a variety of clinical and educational contexts. The BSc in Speech Language Pathology is a coeducational program, with joint female and male classes. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).

Through core courses, technical electives, and either a research thesis or a practicum project, graduates are prepared for professional roles in industry, research, and academia, supporting the UAEU Strategic Plan and advancing technological innovation and digital transformation in the region</p>

The program prepares graduates for senior leadership roles and staff appointments in national and international headquarters.</p>

All admitted students will initially be enrolled in the non-thesis track. DESCRIPTION: The Bachelor of Finance and Banking offered by the Department of Economics and Finance aims to equip students with a solid understanding of finance theories, financial analysis, and recent challenges in the financial industry. Driven by the market need for finance graduates with a good knowledge of the contemporary issues and challenges facing the UAE and the global world, such as technological disruption, sustainable finance, ethical finance, and interconnectedness among global financial markets, coupled with complex financial products and trading strategies. The program provides the graduates with a competitive edge, allowing them to compete and fit into the current dynamics of the job market. Accordingly, it has two concentrations: Financial Management and Financial Technologies (FinTech). Students should choose one concentration. Topics covered in the program include, among others: Sustainability in Finance, Alternative Finance, Insurance and Regulation, Finance Analytics, Financial Technologies I (FinTech 1), Financial Technologies 2 (FinTech 2), Research in FinTech, Research in Banking and Finance, Investment and Portfolio Analysis, Islamic Finance and Banking, and Derivatives Securities. Overall, the program allows students to effectively apply the acquired knowledge and skills, which are important in many businesses and government agencies, and engages them in significant analyses of real-world problems. In doing so, it prepares graduates for decision-making positions in government, corporations, and financial services firms.

## Bachelor of Science in Data Science and Artificial Intelligence — published program-finder record
Record program: Bachelor of Science in Data Science and Artificial Intelligence
Record college: College of Information Technology
Record qualification: bachelor
Record degreeLevel: undergraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/bachelor-of-science-in-data-science-and-artificial-intelligence
TITLE: Bachelor of Science in Data Science and Artificial Intelligence
TITLE_AR: بكالوريوس العلوم في علم البيانات والذكاء الاصطناعي
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Information Technology
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية تقنية المعلومات
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Computer Science and Software Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: مسار تطوير البرمجيات
PROGRAM_ID: 23022
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: Data and artificial intelligence are driving transformative changes across every sector of the economy, unlocking new possibilities for solving complex challenges. The interdisciplinary BS in Data Science and Artificial Intelligence (DSAI) program is designed to prepare students for this data-driven, AI-enhanced world by integrating the three pillars of data science computational, mathematical, and statistical thinking with a strong foundation in AI. This cohesive, transdisciplinary curriculum, coupled with immersive internship experiences, equips students with a comprehensive understanding of data science and AI concepts, tools, and methods.(1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”). The program places a strong emphasis on data science and AI, from foundational theories to advanced applications in machine learning, data mining, and predictive analytics. Students will learn to harness AI to uncover hidden patterns within data, enabling them to derive actionable insights and develop innovative solutions to real-world problems. This focus on AI, combined with a practical, hands-on approach, ensures that students develop a robust skill set to thrive in the rapidly evolving fields of data science and AI. By bridging theory and practice, the BS DSAI program enables students to gain mastery not only in data analysis but also in AI techniques such as machine learning, natural language processing, and computer vision. These skills are critical for success in high-demand roles across industries. The program’s unique emphasis on interdisciplinarity and real-world problem-solving prepares students for the expanding range of opportunities in data science and AI. The rise of AI has fueled an explosive demand for professionals skilled in both data science and AI. While high-tech companies are at the forefront of this demand, sectors beyond technology including healthcare, finance, and government are increasingly seeking AI-savvy data scientists. In alignment with the UAE’s digital transformation goals, the BS DSAI program addresses this critical need, equipping graduates with the skills necessary to excel in a knowledge and AI-driven economy. Graduates of the DSAI program will emerge as well-rounded professionals, capable of leveraging both data science and AI to drive innovation and solve complex problems. With these advanced skills, they will be prepared to take on leading-edge roles in data science, AI, and data analytics across diverse industries and sectors globally. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).
STUDYPLAN_URL: https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cit/pdf/cit_bsc_data_science_and_artificial_intelligence_study_plan.pdf

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Clinical Research - I — published program-finder record — continuation 2
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Clinical Research - I
Record college: College of Medicine and Health Sciences
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-clinical-research--i
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Credit Bearing Micro-Credential (CBMC) in Clinical Research - I is a 7-credit hour program designed to equip healthcare professionals with foundational competencies in clinical research, including research fundamentals, basic biostatistical methods, and research methodology. Teaching is delivered through intensive face-to-face sessions, including team-based learning (TBL), scheduled to accommodate working professionals. This CBMC originates from the Master of Science (MSc) in Clinical Research offered by the College of Medicine and Health Sciences, UAE University, and comprises the first semester of the MSc curriculum. Graduates who achieve a minimum cumulative GPA of 3.0 out of 4.0 may stack their credits towards the full MSc degree. Admission requirements align with the MSc program, with a minimum undergraduate GPA of 2.5 or equivalent. Applicants with a GPA below 3.0 must achieve an overall GPA of 3.0 in both CBMC I and CBMC II to be admitted to the MSc program and stack their credits.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>A Bachelors degree in life sciences, medicine (MBBS, MD, MBBCh), pharmacy, nursing, or a comparable health-related discipline from an accredited institution</li> <li>GPA for the intake to CBMC in clinical research is equal to or higher than 2.5, or its equivalent.</li> <li>An applicant with an undergraduate cumulative GPA less than 3 must achieve an overall GPA of 3.0 on a 4.0 scale in both CBMC I and CBMC II in clinical research to be admitted to the MSc. program and stack the CBMCs</li> <li>English language proficiency: IELTS 6.0 (Academic) or equivalent</li> <li>Successful completion of an interview with the Admissions Committee</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC in Clinical Research I is designed for healthcare professionals seeking structured, university-accredited training in clinical research without committing to a full two-year Master&#39;s degree. The program is particularly suited for physicians, nurses, pharmacists, allied health practitioners, and research coordinators working in clinical or healthcare settings who wish to strengthen their research competencies alongside their professional practice. Ideal candidates include clinicians early in their careers seeking foundational research skills, experienced practitioners aiming to integrate evidence-based approaches into their work, and professionals exploring clinical research as a potential career pathway before committing to advanced postgraduate study. The program also appeals to those who may wish to pursue the full MSc in Clinical Research at a later stage, using the CBMC as a stackable credential towards the Master&#39;s degree. Given the flexible evening and weekend delivery format, the CBMC is well-suited for full-time employees in hospitals, research centres, and healthcare organizations across the UAE who require scheduling that accommodates their professional commitments.</p>

## Master of Science in Physics — published program-finder record
Record program: Master of Science in Physics
Record college: College of Science
Record qualification: master
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/master-of-science-in-physics
TITLE: Master of Science in Physics
TITLE_AR: ماجستير العلوم في الفيزياء
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Science
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية العلوم
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Physics
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الفيزياء
PROGRAM_ID: 23036
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Department of Physics offers a restructured Master of Science (M.Sc.) in Physics, a 30 credit hour (CH) program available in both full-time and part-time formats to accommodate a diverse student body, including in-service teachers, industry professionals, and those pursuing doctoral or research-intensive careers. This revised program introduces a dual-track model that maintains academic rigor while enhancing flexibility and career relevance. The first track, a strengthened Research (Thesis) Track, requires 21 CH of coursework plus 9 CH dedicated to research and the completion of an M.Sc. thesis. Within these 21 CH, students take 12 CH of core courses and 9 CH of elective courses, allowing specialization in specific physics topics. Progress in this track is overseen by a research supervisor (and co-supervisors as needed) and a thesis defense committee. The second track, a new Coursework-Based (Non-Thesis) Track, also totals 30 CH but replaces the thesis with advanced coursework and a culminating capstone project to professional applications. In this track, students complete 24 CH of coursework (including 12 CH core and 12 CH electives) plus a 6 CH capstone project (compulsory) that emphasizes practical or industrial applications of physics. Both tracks are fee-based and open to all qualified applicants who meet the admission requirements, ensuring that graduates are well-prepared either for PhD studies and advanced research careers or for direct entry into the national workforce in education, industry, and other professional sectors. </p>

PROGRAM_ID: 23057
AWARD_LEVEL: Master

DESCRIPTION: <p>The Master of Science (MSc) in Mathematics is a 30-credit-hour graduate program offered by the Department of Mathematical Sciences, College of Science, United Arab Emirates University. The program provides advanced training in pure and applied mathematics, emphasizing rigorous theoretical foundations, analytical reasoning, mathematical modeling, and scholarly inquiry.<br /> The revised program introduces a dual-track structure consisting of a Thesis Track and a Non-Thesis Track. The Thesis Track is designed for students pursuing research-intensive careers and doctoral studies, requiring original research and a formal thesis defense. The Non-Thesis Track is intended for working professionals and students seeking advanced mathematical depth without a research thesis requirement. It culminates in a structured Capstone Project that integrates advanced coursework knowledge into a comprehensive academic analysis.<br /> Both tracks maintain identical academic standards in core coursework and preserve the overall 30-credit-hour requirement, while offering differentiated culminating experiences aligned with students&rsquo; academic and professional goals.</p>

DESCRIPTION: Chemical Engineering is concerned with the manufacturing of products from laboratory bench-scale testing to full production through deep knowledge of fluid mechanics, heat transfer, mass transfer, chemical reaction kinetics, equipment design, plant design, process dynamics and control as well as process safety, economics, and management. It has an impact on essentially everything on our daily life from food processing to producing pharmaceutical drugs, generating fuels and even the manufacturing of silicon chips and other microelectronics. At the Chemical and Petroleum Engineering Department, we strive to help students see how a Chemical Engineering degree can accomplish their dreams and we establish the means to make it happen. The Chemical Engineering undergraduate program in the College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University is accredited by the Engineering Accreditation Commission of ABET. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).
STUDYPLAN_URL: https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/eng/pdf/coe_bsc_chemical_engineering_study_plan.pdf

DESCRIPTION: Civil and Environmental Engineering is a broad field of engineering that deals with planning, design, construction and maintenance of structures, bridges and public works as they relate to earth, water and air, or civilization and their processes. Civil Engineering profession dominates every aspect of our life in one way or the other. The current economic prosperity in the UAE is based, to a great extent, on the excellent infrastructure and civic works developed by Civil Engineers. Civil Engineering is the oldest engineering discipline after Military Engineering. It deals with structures, bridges, construction management, highways, traffic, geotechnical, water supply and distribution networks, sewer and disaster mitigation. Environmental Engineering focuses on the quality and sustainability of the three main environmental elements; soil, water and air. The Department is keen to always provide the highest possible quality of higher education, scientific research, and community service. The Civil Engineering undergraduate program in the College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University is accredited by the Engineering Accreditation Commission of ABET. PROGRAM_ID: 23061
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: Petroleum engineering refers to the subsurface engineering activities related to the production of hydrocarbons, which can be either crude oil or gas. Petroleum Engineering focuses on maximizing economic recovery of hydrocarbons from subsurface reservoirs and estimation of the recoverable volume of this resource using a detailed understanding of the physical behavior of Oil, water and gas within porous rock at very high pressure. Petroleum Engineering requires a good knowledge of many other related disciplines, such as Geology, Petrophysics, Geophysics, and Petroleum Geology. Improvements in computer modeling, materials and the application of statistics, probability analysis have drastically improved the toolbox of the petroleum engineer in recent decades. The Petroleum Engineering undergraduate program in the College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University is accredited by the Engineering Accreditation Commission of ABET. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).

DESCRIPTION: Mechanical engineering is one of the broadest and oldest branches of engineering and can require work that ranges from the design and manufacture of very fine and sensitive instruments with micro and nano scales, to the design and fabrication of huge power plants. The ME program emphasizes a fundamental approach to engineering in which the student learns to identify needs, define problems and apply basic principles and techniques to obtain a solution. This philosophy is incorporated in the classroom lectures, laboratory activities, design projects and research. ME graduates are expected to deal with moving devices and complex systems. Students learn about materials, design, manufacturing, solid and fluid mechanics, thermodynamics, heat transfer, control, and instrumentation, to understand mechanical systems. Specialized ME subjects include energy conversion, energy management, air conditioning, turbumachinery, composite materials and materials processing, combustion, fracture mechanics, selected topics in mechatronics and vibration, control engineering, introduction to robotics, selected topics in manufacturing and design, maintenance engineering, biomechanics and selected topics in bioengineering. The Mechanical Engineering undergraduate program in the College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University is accredited by the Engineering Accreditation Commission of ABET, http://www.abet.org. ## Doctor of Philosophy in Environmental Engineering — published program-finder record
Record program: Doctor of Philosophy in Environmental Engineering
Record college: College of Engineering
Record qualification: doctorate
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/doctor-of-philosophy-in-environmental-engineering
TITLE: Doctor of Philosophy in Environmental Engineering
TITLE_AR: دكتوراه الفلسفة في الهندسة البيئية
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Engineering
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الهندسة
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Civil & Environmental Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الهندسة المدنية والبينية
PROGRAM_ID: 23073
AWARD_LEVEL: Doctorate
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>As one of the fastest-growing fields in both developed and developing countries, new methods and technologies are constantly emerging in environmental engineering. The Ph.D. Program in Environmental Engineering is in response to the national and international dire need for developing innovative solutions for environmental protection and combating climate change. This program prepares students to be capable of providing leadership and necessary technical expertise to governmental and private sectors. In this research program, students will implement updates of all aspects of environmental engineering including tools, techniques and regulations. Air quality control, water sanitation and its distribution, water treatment, waste pollutants sources and effects should also be covered. Candidates will draw attention to solid waste collection and transfer operations, hazardous waste remediation and assessment of environmental impacts. The offered program is interdisciplinary within different engineering disciplines. It accepts students with backgrounds in any relevant Engineering program. The Ph.D. degree in Environmental Engineering requires successful completion of graduate coursework and credit hours in research in addition to passing the qualification requirements, and dissertation defense exam.</p>

AWARD_LEVEL: Master

DESCRIPTION: <p>The program for Master of Science in Clinical Research (Clin Res MSc) is a two-year graduate-degree program designed to provide candidates with in-depth knowledge and practical skills to plan and implement a clinical research program, and translate their research findings into actions that will inform clinical practice and improve health and patient outcomes. Students must complete all planned courses totaling 30 credit hours, and successfully defend their research thesis, for graduation. This comprises 17 credit hours of compulsory courses, 4 credit hours of elective courses, and 9 credit hours for the thesis research project. In addition to formal classroom teaching, students are expected to spend time on self-directed study and homework assignments. For every hour spent in the classroom, students should aim to spend two hours on self-directed study.</p>

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in AI for Intelligent Energy Systems — published program-finder record

Record college: College of Information Technology
Record qualification: microcredential

Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-ai-for-intelligent-energy-systems
TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in AI for Intelligent Energy Systems
TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة في الذكاء الاصطناعي للأنظمة الذكية للطاقة
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Information Technology
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية تقنية المعلومات
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Computer and Network Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: مسار الشبكات
PROGRAM_ID: 23082
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in AI for Intelligent Energy Systems is a 6-credit undergraduate micro credential that prepares students to apply AI data engineering methods to modern energy systems. The CBMC is intended for undergraduate students from computing, engineering, science, analytics, and related disciplines who have prior knowledge of introductory AI, Python programming, and basic statistics. It is especially suited to students interested in AI applications in sustainability, smart infrastructure, and energy analytics. The micro credential combines an existing major elective in AI Data Engineering with a newly developed course that explores AI applications in energy, including energy forecasting, smart buildings, anomaly detection, and intelligent energy decision support. The CBMC originates from the Bachelor of Science in Computer and AI Engineering and is proposed as a non-stackable micro credential.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>Required prerequisites iclude: <ul> <li>CENG 110 (or GEIT 113, ELEC 290, or equivalent)</li> <li>CSBP 110 (or CENG 230, or equivalent)</li> <li>STAT 210 (or equivalent)</li> </ul> </li> <li>Equivalent courses are determined by the offering department</li> <li>Students are expected to have prior knowledge in: <ul> <li>Introductory AI</li> <li>Programming</li> <li>Basic statistics</li> </ul> </li> <li>Enrollment in the second course requires passing CENG 400 AI Data Engineering.</li> <li>Courses taken for the CBMC cannot be counted toward a student&rsquo;s degree program.</li> <li>If overlap occurs, students must take alternative approved courses to fulfill degree requirements.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC is intended for undergraduate students from computing, engineering, science, analytics, and related disciplines who have prior knowledge of introductory AI, Python programming, and basic statistics. It is especially suited to students interested in AI applications in sustainability, smart infrastructure, and energy analytics.</p>

DESCRIPTION: The Coordinated Program in Dietetics (CPD) offered by the Department of Nutrition and Health (DNH), College of Medicine and Health Sciences aims to prepare graduates who are competent entry-level dietitians. The program&#39;s mission is to prepare competent graduates who are highly qualified entry-level dietitians, to improve the nutritional well-being and health of the UAE population. The program goals are (1) to prepare graduates to be competent, entry-level dietitians and (2) to prepare graduates who demonstrate leadership and a commitment to community service. The CPD at UAEU is accredited as a Foreign Dietitian Education Program (FDE) by the Accreditation Council for Education in Nutrition and Dietetics (ACEND) of the Academy of Nutrition and Dietetics (AND), 120 South Riverside Plaza, Suite 2190, Chicago, IL 60606, 800-877-1600, ext. 5400; website:(http://www.eatright.org/ACEND/). The program prepares students to achieve the ACEND Core Knowledge and Competencies for an entry-level dietitian through academic courses and 750 hours of supervised practice experience which includes 550 hours of supervised practice rotations in various training facilities. Effective January 1, 2024, the Commission on Dietetic Registration (CDR) will require a minimum of a masters degree to be eligible to take the credentialing exam to become a registered dietitian nutritionist (RDN). In order to be approved for registration examination eligibility with a bachelors degree, an individual must meet all eligibility requirements and be submitted into CDR9;s Registration Eligibility Processing System (REPS) before 12:00 midnight Central Time, December 31, 2023. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”). (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).

TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة هندسة أنظمة الذكاء الاصطناعي التوليدي
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Information Technology
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية تقنية المعلومات
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Computer and Network Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: مسار الشبكات
PROGRAM_ID: 23085
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>This graduate 6-credit CBMC equips learners with practical knowledge and applied skills in generative AI and the engineering, deployment, and operation of AI systems. It targets graduates and professionals seeking upskilling in production-ready AI, with emphasis on prompt-driven and multimodal applications, evaluation, deployment pipelines, scalability, governance, and responsible implementation. The CBMC is designed for learners interested in advanced digital services, enterprise AI transformation, and innovation priorities in the UAE. The CBMC is derived from the MSc in IoT and AI Engineering and is intended to be attractive to learners seeking focused graduate-level preparation in high-demand AI capabilities.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>&bull;Applicants intending to stack the CBMC toward the MSc in IoT and AI Engineering must satisfy the applicable admission requirements of the College of Graduate Studies (CGS) and the MSc program in effect at the time of application. Please refer to the official CGS admission requirements at: https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/cgs/admission.shtml</li> <li>Applicants who do not intend to stack the CBMC toward a degree must hold a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in computer engineering, Computer Science, or a related field with a minimum GPA of 2.5, or possess relevant industry experience acceptable to the offering department. Applicants must also have prior preparation in programming and in artificial intelligence and machine learning.</li> <li>A student may not be enrolled simultaneously in the CBMC and the MSc in IoT and AI Engineering. Accordingly, the following cases apply: <ul> <li>A student may enroll in and complete the CBMC prior to enrolling in the MSc program.</li> <li>Additional preparation or departmental approval may be required in some cases.</li> </ul> </li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC is intended for graduate students and industry professionals in computing, computer engineering, artificial intelligence, data science, electrical engineering, and related disciplines. It is particularly suited to learners with prior exposure to programming and introductory artificial intelligence who wish to develop knowledge and practical skills in generative AI and AI systems engineering. The CBMC prepares learners to apply modern generative AI methods and to support the engineering, deployment, and management of scalable AI systems for real-world applications.</p>

DESCRIPTION: <p>The Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Environmental Engineering Essentials (CBMC-EEE) is designed to provide students with core knowledge and applied competencies in environmental engineering, with emphasis on environmental chemistry and microbiology, risk assessment, pollution control and treatment, and sustainability. The CBMC integrates fundamental concepts with analytical and design approaches to address contemporary environmental challenges, including water and wastewater treatment, air quality management, waste management, and environmental impact assessment. The CBMC-EEE targets undergraduate students&nbsp;as well as holders of a bachelor&rsquo;s degree,&nbsp;except currently enrolled UAEU Civil Engineering students.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>Existing Undergraduate Students: <ul> <li>UAEU students must have a Cumulative GPA of 2.5 or higher at the time of application.</li> <li>UAEU students must have scored a grade of B or higher in both courses CHEM111 and CHEM175.</li> <li>Students from outside UAEU should meet equivalent requirements, which will be evaluated on a case-by-case basis.</li> </ul> </li> <li>Holders of a Bachelor&rsquo;s Degree: <ul> <li>Learners should have completed a bachelor&rsquo;s degree and meet equivalent requirements, which will be evaluated on a case-by-case basis.</li> </ul> </li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC-EEE targets UAEU undergraduate students excluding currently enrolled Civil Engineering students at UAEU. Additionally, it is open to professionals holding a bachelor&rsquo;s degree as well as undergraduate students from outside UAEU, subject to meeting equivalent requirements.</p>

DESCRIPTION: The undergraduate B.Sc. program in Statistics and Data Analytics at UAEU introduces the concepts, methods, and tools of collecting, processing, and analyzing data. The objective is to discover hidden patterns in data and generate actionable insights. Building on the fundamental concepts of probability and statistical inference (i.e., estimation & hypothesis testing), the program provides the fundamental background, as well as the modern techniques for statistics and data analytics. Two distinctive features of the program are: the emphasis on real-world applications; and the enrichment of lecture materials through practical experience with state-of-the-art computer software and modeling languages. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).

TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة في موارد الأرض والطاقة المستدامة
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Science

DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الجيولوجيا

AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>This 6-credit-hour Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential (CBMC) in Sustainable Earth and Energy Resources provides focused graduate-level training in environmental and exploration geochemistry, climate evolution, geoinformatics, and environmental hazards.
Learners develop advanced knowledge and applied skills to explain earth-system processes, analyze geoscientific datasets, evaluate environmental risks, and support sustainable resource and energy management.
The CBMC is offered through two tracks: (i) a non-stackable standalone track for learners seeking focused upskilling without admission to a full graduate degree; and (ii) a stackable track for eligible learners who can be admitted to the Master of Science in Geosciences, subject to College of Graduate Studies (CGS), Admission Office, program, and university regulations.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <p>Applications to the CBMC will follow the relevant UAEU admission cycles, deadlines, and review procedures.
Applicants must be assigned to one of two tracks at admission or registration: the non-stackable standalone CBMC track or the stackable CBMC track.
Stackable status is not automatic and is available only to students who satisfy the relevant CGS, Admission Office, program, and university regulations for the associated degree pathway.</p> <p><strong>Stackable towards a degree:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Holders of a Bachelor&rsquo;s degree: Admission requirements for students with a Bachelor&rsquo;s degree in Geosciences or a related discipline (same admission requirements of UAEU Master&rsquo;s Programs).</li> <li>Undergraduate students: <ul> <li>Completion of 75 credit hours + minimum GPA (3.0) + IELTS 6.0</li> <li>To stack towards an MSc.
in Geosciences degree, students should achieve the admission requirements of UAEU Master&rsquo;s Programs.</li> </ul> </li> </ul> <p><strong>Non-stackable towards a degree:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Holders of a Bachelor&rsquo;s Degree <ul> <li>Hold a Bachelor&#39;s degree or equivalent in physical sciences, geology, geography, engineering, information technology, environmental science, or a closely related discipline, as determined by the program committee and in accordance with College and University regulations.</li> <li>Minimum cumulative GPA of 2.5 on a 4.0 scale or equivalent, subject to institutional regulations.

DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم اللغات وآدابها
PROGRAM_ID: 23132
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: The program responds to a growing demand for professional translators well-equipped to meet the needs of the multinational society of the UAE. The program is designed to provide theoretical and practical training for students to become professional translators, and to introduce them to the requirements of specialized translation and interpreting. The curriculum familiarizes students with problems they may face in English-into-Arabic and Arabic-into-English translation and interpreting and how to solve them. The curriculum includes various specialized courses such as legal, scientific and medical, journalistic, administrative, game localization and audiovisual translation. Students can also develop their skills in community, consecutive and simultaneous interpreting, three types of interpreting much required in the job market today. Recent developments in technologies including AI-assisted translation as well as professional aspects related to the fields of translation and interpreting will also be covered. The program offers internship opportunities for students to train in different institutions around the UAE.

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Renewable Energy Technologies — published program-finder record
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Renewable Energy Technologies
Record college: College of Science
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: undergraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-renewable-energy-technologies
TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Renewable Energy Technologies
TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدةفي تقنيات الطاقة المتجددة
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Science
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية العلوم
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Physics
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الفيزياء
PROGRAM_ID: 23133
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The undergraduate Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential (CBMC) program in Renewable Energy Technologies provides specialized, advanced-level training in the field without requiring students to commit to a full degree program. It is designed to equip learners with a comprehensive foundation in renewable energy, energy storage, hydrogen production, and low-carbon systems. Participants will develop a robust skill set encompassing applied energy physics, renewable energy technologies, electrochemical storage, hydrogen systems, energy materials, and sustainability-oriented energy analysis. The program thereby prepares students for research, innovation, and professional practice in renewable energy and related technological sectors. This CBMC program is open to students enrolled in STEM disciplines. The credit hours earned through this program are awarded in addition to the standard credit requirements for graduation from an undergraduate bachelor’s degree program. The 9 credit hours earned through this program will not be double-counted toward the credit requirements of the undergraduate degree. The CBMC program is a stand-alone, non-stackable credential and cannot be combined with other academic credentials for credit toward a degree.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <p>The CBMC in Renewable Energy Technologies is open to undergraduate students currently pursuing a bachelor&rsquo;s degree at the university, as well as professionals working in industry. Undergraduate students may be admitted to the program if they have a minimum GPA of 2.5 at the time of application and obtain approval from their Department and Physics Department.</p> <p>Applicants working in industry must meet the following admission requirements:</p> <ul> <li>A bachelor&rsquo;s degree in science, engineering, or a related discipline from an accredited institution recognized by the UAE Ministry of Education, with a minimum cumulative GPA of 2.5 on a 4.0 scale or its equivalent.</li> <li>A minimum IELTS Academic score of 5.5, or an equivalent qualification, obtained within two years prior to the application date.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC is available to students enrolled in STEM disciplines, including physics, mathematics, chemistry, engineering, or related fields. It is intended for undergraduate students seeking to strengthen their knowledge and competencies in renewable energy technologies, whether to support career development or to prepare for advanced graduate study. The program is also suitable for early-career professionals who wish to improve or reskill in response to the evolving demands of the UAE&rsquo;s industrial and technology sectors.</p>

DESCRIPTION: <p>The College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University offers an Accelerated Bachelor&ndash;Master option in Civil Engineering, designed to enable outstanding undergraduate students to complete their M.Sc.
degree in a shorter timeframe.</p> <p>Through the Bachelor&ndash;Master Accelerated Option, students may begin earning graduate-level credits while still fulfilling the requirements of the B.Sc.
degree.
Up to two Master&rsquo;s-level courses (6 credit hours) may be double-counted toward both the Bachelor&rsquo;s and Master&rsquo;s degrees.</p> <h3>Objectives</h3> <ol> <li>Make it easier for B.Sc.
students to pursue graduate studies.</li> <li>Provide advanced knowledge, skills, and attributes to become independent learners and leaders.</li> <li>Open opportunities for successful careers at the forefront of industrial development, governmental, consulting, or academic areas.</li> <li>Shorten the time required to earn a graduate degree.</li> <li>Better prepare for the current and future requirements of professional registration within the U.A.E.
and internationally.</li> <li>Foster a tradition of graduate research activity.</li> </ol> <h3>Credit Requirements</h3> <p>After completing the B.Sc.
degree, students must apply to the M.Sc.
program through the UAEU website.
The M.Sc.
degree requires the completion of 30 graduate credit hours distributed as follows:</p> <p><strong>Thesis Option:</strong></p> <p>21 coursework credits + 9 thesis credits</p> <p><strong>Non-Thesis Option:</strong></p> <p>24 coursework credits + 6 technical project credits</p> <p>Under the integrated Bachelor-Master Accelerated option, outstanding junior and senior undergraduate students may double count up to two Master&rsquo;s-level courses (maximum 6 CHs) toward both their B.Sc.
and M.Sc.
degree requirements.
These double counting Master-level courses will replace approved elective courses in the B.Sc.
degree requirements.</p> <p>In addition, BS&ndash;MS students may take 1&ndash;2 extra graduate courses during their Bachelor&rsquo;s study.
These additional courses will count only toward the M.Sc.
degree.</p> <h3>General Admission Requirements</h3> <ol> <li>Advanced level (Junior or Senior) B.Sc.
status with a minimum of 75&nbsp;CH of the B.Sc.

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Intelligent Infrastructure Systems — published program-finder record
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Intelligent Infrastructure Systems
Record college: College of Engineering
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-intelligent-infrastructure-systems
TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Intelligent Infrastructure Systems
TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة في أنظمة البنية التحتية الذكية
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Engineering
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الهندسة
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Civil & Environmental Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الهندسة المدنية والبينية
PROGRAM_ID: 23140
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The 6-credit-hour graduate Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Intelligent Infrastructure Systems (CBMC-IIS) aims to provide Engineering students with knowledge, skills, and practical applications of Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) and smart infrastructure systems. Students will also learn the principles and applications of Digital Twins (DTs) in the planning, design, construction, and operation of integrated infrastructure systems, including transportation and urban assets. CBMC-IIS addresses infrastructure challenges and supports the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs), particularly the goals of building resilient infrastructure and sustainable cities and communities. It is open to: (i) current undergraduate engineering students fulfilling the admission requirements of CBMC-IIS, and (ii) leaners holding a Bachelor&rsquo;s Degree in civil engineering or a related discipline.</p> <h3>Admission</h3> <p><strong>Current engineering undergraduate students:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Students should satisfy the admission requirements of the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option</li> <li>Successful Completion of STAT 210 Probability and Statistics, GENG 230 Computer Programming, MECH 305 Mechanics of Materials, and CIVL 310 Structural Analysis.</li> </ul> <p><strong>Holders of a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in Civil Engineering or a related discipline</strong></p> <ul> <li>Learners should satisfy the admission requirements of the UAEU Engineering Master&rsquo;s Program</li> </ul> <p><strong>Non-stackable towards a degree:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Learners should hold a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in civil engineering or a related discipline and have at least one year of work experience following the completion of their bachelor&rsquo;s degree. Such applications will be considered on a case-by-case basis.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p> The CBMC-IIS targets learners holding a bachelor’s degree in civil engineering or a related discipline, as well as meritorious UAEU undergraduate engineering students who are willing to develop knowledge and skills in AI-assisted structural health monitoring and digital twin technologies to support infrastructure assessment, predictive maintenance, and informed decision-making throughout the infrastructure lifecycle. </p>

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Environmental Risk and Impact Assessment — published program-finder record
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Environmental Risk and Impact Assessment
Record college: College of Engineering
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate

TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Environmental Risk and Impact Assessment
TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة في تقييم المخاطر والأثر
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Engineering
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الهندسة
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Civil & Environmental Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الهندسة المدنية والبينية
PROGRAM_ID: 23142
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Environmental Risk and Impact Assessment (CBMC-ERIA) provides core competencies in environmental impact assessment and risk analysis. It integrates regulatory frameworks, impact evaluation, probabilistic risk modeling, and techno-economic analysis to address environmental challenges. Artificial intelligence and data analytics are used as advanced tools to support modeling, prediction, and decision-making. The CBMC-ERIA targets current undergraduate students fulfilling the admission requirements of the CBMC-ERIA and learners holding a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in a related discipline.</p> <h3>Admission</h3> <p><strong>Current undergraduate students:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Students should satisfy the admission requirements of the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option</li> <li>Successful Completion of CIVL 270 &ndash; Introduction to Environmental Engineering and GENG 315 Engineering Economics</li> </ul> <p><strong>Holders of a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in Civil/Environmental Engineering or a related discipline:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Learners should satisfy the admission requirements of the UAEU Master&rsquo;s Program</li> </ul> <p><strong>Non-stackable towards a degree:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Learners should hold a bachelor’s degree in a related discipline and have at least one year of work experience following the completion of their bachelor’s degree. Such applications will be considered on a case-by-case basis.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC-ERIA targets learners holding a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in diverse fields, including civil, environmental, chemical, and related engineering and scientific disciplines, as well as meritorious UAEU undergraduate students who are willing to develop competencies in environmental risk assessment, impact evaluation, and sustainable finance, supported by data analytics and AI-based tools.</p>

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Accounting Foundations — published program-finder record
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Accounting Foundations
Record college: College of Business and Economics
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: undergraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-accounting-foundations
TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Accounting Foundations
TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة في أسس المحاسبة
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Business and Economics
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الادارة و الاقتصاد
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Department of Accounting
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم المحاسبة
PROGRAM_ID: 23143
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The CBMC in Accounting Foundations is a 6-credit, stand-alone, non-stackable credit-bearing micro-credential that provides non-business students with essential knowledge and practical skills in financial and management accounting. Students will learn how to record and interpret accounting transactions, prepare and analyze basic financial statements, and apply cost and management accounting tools to support planning, budgeting, cost control, and decision-making. The micro-credential develops foundational accounting competencies that help students understand financial information and apply accounting concepts in practical business contexts.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>Applicants should hold a recognized no-business bachelor’s degree or equivalent qualification approved by the University.</li> <li>High-achieving non-business undergraduate students with a GPA of 3.0 or above and have completed 75 credit hours or more.</li> <li>Because the CBMC has no formal prerequisites, the CBMC may be offered to non-business graduates and other eligible learners seeking entry-level accounting preparation.</li> <li>As a stand-alone CBMC, applicants are not required to meet admission requirements for a specific degree program; however, any future use of these courses toward a degree would remain subject to university admission and credit-recognition policies.</li> <li>Currently enrolled College of Business students or graduates are not eligible to enroll in this CBMC, as the micro-credential is designed to provide foundational accounting knowledge for non-business learners.</li> <li>Courses completed as part of this CBMC will not be double-counted toward degree requirements. Current UAEU degree students may be subject to enrollment restrictions if any CBMC course is already included as a required or elective course in their degree program. Enrollment will be reviewed in accordance with applicable UAEU policies and CBMC requirements.</li> <li>Admission to the CBMC will follow the UAEU undergraduate admissions cycle. This is with intake available in both the Fall and Spring semesters, subject to university admission timelines and offering arrangements.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC is open to non-business graduates, career changers, aspiring entrepreneurs, early-career professionals, and other lifelong learners who seek foundational knowledge in accounting. It is also open to high-achieving undergraduate students if they have a GPA of 3.0 or above and have completed 75 credit hours or more. The credential is also suitable for applicants seeking structured preparation for professional development, career transition, or later advanced study in accounting or related business disciplines.</p>

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Sustainable Resource Management in the Built Environment — published program-finder record
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Sustainable Resource Management in the Built Environment
Record college: College of Engineering
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: undergraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-sustainable-resource-management-in-the-built-environment
TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Sustainable Resource Management in the Built Environment
TITLE_AR: وحدات التعلم قصيرة المدى المعتمدة في إدارة الموارد المستدامة في البيئة العمرانية
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Engineering
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الهندسة
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Architectural Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الهندسة المعمارية
PROGRAM_ID: 23145
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential (CBMC) in Sustainable Resource Management and AI Applications in the Built Environment is designed to develop essential competencies in environmental sustainability, resource efficiency, performance-based design, and AI-supported analytical and visualization approaches. The program equips learners with the knowledge and AI-supported analytical skills&nbsp;required to assess environmental impacts across the life cycle of buildings and infrastructure, and to formulate effective strategies for resource-efficient design strategies and operational practices, with particular emphasis on water management in hot-arid environments.</p> <p>Learners will develop a solid foundation in life cycle assessment (LCA), environmental performance evaluation, and sustainable landscape and water management practices. The program emphasizes analytical thinking, AI-assisted decision-making, sustainability-oriented design approaches, and evidence-based decision-making to address complex environmental challenges in the built environment.</p> <p>This micro-credential is intended for engineering students and professionals seeking to strengthen their expertise in sustainability assessment and resource management, particularly in arid and water-scarce regions.</p> <h3>Admission</h3> <ul> <li>UAEU undergraduate students who have successfully completed at least one year of study with a minimum GPA of &ldquo;2.5&rdquo; and satisfy the prerequisite requirements of the constituent courses.</li> <li>Non-degree learners and professionals holding a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in engineering, Architecture, Construction, or a related discipline who wish to undertake the CBMC for continuing professional upskilling and development.</li> <li>All applicants must satisfy the prerequisite requirements of the constituent courses and the English language requirements in accordance with UAEU regulations.</li> <li>Equivalent academic background or relevant professional experience may be accepted.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <ul> <li>Undergraduate students in Architectural Engineering</li> <li>Undergraduate students from related Engineering disciplines.</li> <li>Non-degree learners seeking undergraduate-level professional development.</li> <li>Engineering and construction professionals seeking to enhance competencies in sustainable resource management and AI-supported environmental assessment.</li> </ul>

## Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Financial Accounting, Auditing, and Taxation — published program-finder record — continuation 2
Record program: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Financial Accounting, Auditing, and Taxation
Record college: College of Business and Economics
Record qualification: microcredential
Record degreeLevel: undergraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/undergraduate/programs/creditbearing-microcredential-in-financial-accounting-auditing-and-taxation
DESCRIPTION: <p>A 9-credit non-stackable undergraduate micro-credential designed to develop applied competence in financial accounting, auditing, and taxation for learners who already possess introductory accounting preparation. The CBMC strengthens the ability to apply IFRS-based financial reporting concepts, understand audit planning and evidence, and address key issues in individual, corporate, and VAT taxation. It is offered as a credit-bearing undergraduate micro-credential for learners seeking advanced accounting upskilling and preparation for licensure-oriented pathways. It may also serve as a progression route for learners who completed the CBMC in Accounting Foundations or an approved equivalent in introductory accounting.</p> <h3>Admission Requirements</h3> <ul> <li>Applicants should hold a recognized bachelor&rsquo;s degree or equivalent qualification. It is also open to high-achieving undergraduate students if they have GPA of 3.0 or above and have completed 75 credit hours or more.</li> <li>Applicants must demonstrate prior completion of introductory financial accounting, such as ACCT 101, the CBMC in Accounting Foundations, or an approved equivalent, because ACCT 200 requires this background.</li> <li>Within the CBMC, ACCT 200 should normally be completed before ACCT 300 and ACCT 301, consistent with the existing prerequisite structure.</li> <li>Currently enrolled accounting students or graduates are not eligible to enroll in this CBMC, as the micro-credential is designed to provide intermediate accounting knowledge for non-accounting learners.</li> <li>Courses completed as part of this CBMC will not be double-counted toward degree requirements. Current UAEU degree students may be subject to enrollment restrictions if any CBMC course is already included as a required or elective course in their degree program. Enrollment will be reviewed in accordance with applicable UAEU policies and CBMC requirements.</li> <li>Admission to the CBMC will follow the UAEU undergraduate admissions cycle. This is with intake available in both the Fall and Spring semesters, subject to university admission timelines and offering arrangements.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <p>The CBMC is intended for learners who already possess introductory accounting preparation and wish to progress into applied financial reporting, auditing, and taxation. It is suitable for non-accounting graduates seeking specialist upskilling, early-career accounting staff, candidates preparing for further study, and lifelong learners interested in a structured advanced accounting pathway. It is also open to high-achieving non-accounting undergraduate students if they have GPA of 3.0 or above and have completed 75 credit hours or more. The CBMC may also serve as a progression route for learners who completed the foundational accounting micro-credential or an equivalent introductory accounting course.</p>

TITLE: Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential in Financial Engineering
