PROGRAM_ID: 23149
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The Credit-Bearing Micro-Credential (CBMC) in Financial Engineering is designed to provide participants with a strong foundation in derivative securities, financial engineering techniques, and risk management practices employed in modern financial markets. The program aims to develop the analytical and practical skills required to understand, value, and utilize financial derivatives for investment, hedging, and risk management purposes.</p> <p>The program comprises 6 credit hours, delivered through two 3-credit-hour courses: Fundamentals of Financial Derivatives and Financial Engineering. It is intended for bachelor&#39;s degree holders from non-finance disciplines seeking to broaden their knowledge of finance and acquire specialized expertise in derivatives and financial risk management. The program is also suitable for professionals seeking advanced knowledge in these areas. In addition, it is open to high-achieving undergraduate students from non-finance disciplines who have completed at least 75 credit hours and attained a minimum cumulative GPA of 3.0. This is a non-stackable, credit-bearing micro-credential program offered as a standalone professional qualification.</p> <h3>Admission</h3> <ul> <li>Bachelor&#39;s degree holders: Students are required to hold a bachelor&rsquo;s degree in a non-finance major.</li> <li>High-achieving undergraduate students: High achiever non-finance bachelor students are allowed to take this CBMC provided that have finished more than 75 credit hours, have GPA above 3.</li> </ul> <h3>Targeted Students</h3> <ul> <li>Bachelor&#39;s degree holders: from non-finance disciplines seeking to broaden their knowledge of finance and acquire specialized expertise in derivatives and financial risk management. The program is also suitable for professionals seeking advanced knowledge in these areas.</li> <li>High-achieving undergraduate students: from non-finance disciplines who have completed at least 75 credit hours, achieved a minimum cumulative GPA of 3.0.</li> </ul>

PROGRAM_ID: 23158
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: The architectural engineering program prepares students to be effective players in shaping a sustainable built environment in the UAE and beyond. Students specializing in Architectural Engineering will explore engineering design, building construction, structures, electrical and mechanical systems and construction management. This makes architectural engineering an ideal profession for individuals with strong math and science skills who are interested in the built environment in general and buildings in particular. The program and department activities reflect an outcomes-oriented approach, adopting hands-on active learning and emphasizing professional competency and skills building while introducing students to innovative approaches to knowledge delivery and use of computational design tools. Teamwork is also a key part of the study of architectural engineering as architectural engineers interact with the other design professionals in the execution of building projects. The Architectural Engineering undergraduate program in the College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University is accredited by the Engineering Accreditation Commission of ABET. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).

## Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Architectural Engineering — published program-finder record
Record program: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Architectural Engineering
Record college: College of Engineering
Record qualification: accelerated

TITLE: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Architectural Engineering
TITLE_AR: خيار البكالوريوس-الماجستير المسرع في الهندسة المعمارية
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Engineering
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الهندسة
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Architectural Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الهندسة المعمارية
PROGRAM_ID: 23176
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University offers an Accelerated Bachelor&ndash;Master option in Architectural Engineering, designed to enable outstanding undergraduate students to complete their M.Sc.
degree in a shorter timeframe.</p> <p>Through the Bachelor&ndash;Master Accelerated Option, students may begin earning graduate-level credits while still fulfilling the requirements of the B.Sc.
degree.
Up to two Master&rsquo;s-level courses (6 credit hours) may be double-counted toward both the Bachelor&rsquo;s and Master&rsquo;s degrees.</p> <h3>Objectives</h3> <ol> <li>Make it easier for B.Sc.
students to pursue graduate studies.</li> <li>Provide advanced knowledge, skills, and attributes to become independent learners and leaders.</li> <li>Open opportunities for successful careers at the forefront of industrial development, governmental, consulting, or academic areas.</li> <li>Shorten the time required to earn a graduate degree.</li> <li>Better prepare for the current and future requirements of professional registration within the U.A.E.
and internationally.</li> <li>Foster a tradition of graduate research activity.</li> </ol> <h3>Credit Requirements</h3> <p>After completing the B.Sc.
degree, students must apply to the M.Sc.
program through the UAEU website.
The M.Sc.
degree requires the completion of 30 graduate credit hours distributed as follows:</p> <p><strong>Thesis Option</strong></p> <p>21 coursework credits + 9 thesis credits</p> <p><strong>Non-Thesis Option</strong></p> <p>24 coursework credits + 6 technical project credits</p> <p>Under the integrated Bachelor-Master Accelerated option, outstanding junior and senior undergraduate students may double count up to two Master&rsquo;s-level courses (maximum 6 CHs) toward both their B.Sc.
and M.Sc.
degree requirements.
These double counting Master-level courses will replace approved elective courses in the B.Sc.
degree requirements.</p> <p>In addition, BS&ndash;MS students may take 1&ndash;2 extra graduate courses during their Bachelor&rsquo;s study.
These additional courses will count only toward the M.Sc.
degree.</p> <h3>General Admission Requirements</h3> <ol> <li>Advanced level (Junior or Senior) B.Sc.
status with a minimum of 75 CH of the B.Sc.

COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية الهندسة
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Chemical & Petroleum Engineering
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم الهندسة الكيمياء والبترول
PROGRAM_ID: 23178
AWARD_LEVEL: Master
LEVEL: Graduate
DESCRIPTION: <p>The College of Engineering at the United Arab Emirates University offers an Accelerated Bachelor&ndash;Master option in Petroleum Engineering, designed to enable outstanding undergraduate students to complete their M.Sc.
degree in a shorter timeframe.</p> <p>Through the Bachelor&ndash;Master Accelerated Option, students may begin earning graduate-level credits while still fulfilling the requirements of the B.Sc.
degree.
Up to two Master&rsquo;s-level courses (6 credit hours) may be double-counted toward both the Bachelor&rsquo;s and Master&rsquo;s degrees.</p> <h3>Objectives</h3> <ol> <li>Make it easier for B.Sc.
students to pursue graduate studies.</li> <li>Provide advanced knowledge, skills, and attributes to become independent learners and leaders.</li> <li>Open opportunities for successful careers at the forefront of industrial development, governmental, consulting, or academic areas.</li> <li>Shorten the time required to earn a graduate degree.</li> <li>Better prepare for the current and future requirements of professional registration within the U.A.E.
and internationally.</li> <li>Foster a tradition of graduate research activity.</li> </ol> <h3>Credit Requirements</h3> <p>After completing the B.Sc.
degree, students must apply to the M.Sc.
program through the UAEU website.
The M.Sc.
degree requires the completion of 30 graduate credit hours distributed as follows:</p> <p><strong>Thesis Option:</strong></p> <p>21 coursework credits + 9 thesis credits</p> <p><strong>Non-Thesis Option:</strong></p> <p>24 coursework credits + 6 technical project credits</p> <p>Under the integrated Bachelor-Master Accelerated option, outstanding junior and senior undergraduate students may double count up to two Master&rsquo;s-level courses (maximum 6 CHs) toward both their B.Sc.
and M.Sc.
degree requirements.
These double counting Master-level courses will replace approved elective courses in the B.Sc.
degree requirements.</p> <p>In addition, BS&ndash;MS students may take 1&ndash;2 extra graduate courses during their Bachelor&rsquo;s study.
These additional courses will count only toward the M.Sc.
degree.</p> <h3>General Admission Requirements</h3> <ol> <li>Advanced level (Junior or Senior) B.Sc.
status with a minimum of 90 CH of the B.Sc.

## Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Petroleum Engineering — published program-finder record — continuation 2
Record program: Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option in Petroleum Engineering
Record college: College of Engineering
Record qualification: accelerated
Record degreeLevel: postgraduate
Record topicId: /catalog/graduate/programs/bachelormaster-accelerated-option-in-petroleum-engineering
program completed with a cumulative GPA of 3.5&nbsp;or higher.</li> <li>Minimum number of CHs to apply for the program is 75</li> <li>Proficiency in English demonstrated by a score of 6.0 on IELTS or equivalent.</li> <li>Acceptance of the responsibility and obligation to pay graduate study fees.</li> </ol> <h3>Early Admission Procedure</h3> <ol> <li>Fill the Bachelor-Master Accelerated Option form and submit to the college for review</li> <li>Submission deadline follows the University Master application deadline.</li> </ol> <h3>Academic Advising Guidelines</h3> <ol> <li>Master level courses form must be filled by the BS-MS student before the first week of the semester.</li> <li>Up to two Master level courses can be double counted for the BS-MS students and will be transferred to their M.Sc.
degrees upon joining the M.Sc.
program.</li> <li>A grade of &ldquo;B&rdquo; or better is required in a course to qualify for the transfer to the M.Sc.
degree.</li> <li>If a BS&ndash;MS student fails to maintain a cumulative GPA of 3.0 or higher in the graduate-level courses, those courses will not be counted toward the M.Sc.
degree.</li> </ol>

TITLE: Bachelor of Arts in English Literature
TITLE_AR: بكالوريوس الآداب في الأدب الانجليزي
COLLEGE_NAME: College of Humanities and Social Sciences
COLLEGE_NAME_AR: كلية العلوم الانسانية والاجتماعية
DEPARTMENT_NAME: Languages and Literature
DEPARTMENT_NAME_AR: قسم اللغات وآدابها
PROGRAM_ID: 23184
AWARD_LEVEL: Undergraduate
LEVEL: Undergraduate
DESCRIPTION: The BA in English Literature seeks to help students develop linguistic and cultural fluency in literatures in English, the current global language, as well as competence in core and advanced critical thinking, analysis, communication, and research. The program aims at producing graduates who will contribute to the cultural, commercial, and intellectual welfare of their home country, and who will foster cultural exchange and understanding regionally and globally. The BA in English Literature prepares students for professional careers in education, research, publishing, business, and many other fields. (1 Credit Hour “CH” is equivalent to 2 ECTS Credit Hours “European Credit Transfer and Accumulation System”).
STUDYPLAN_URL: https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/en/chss/pdf/chss_bsc_english_literature_study_plan.pdf
